# MADHAV DATA PLANE L3 — W0 FIELD / PARTITION CONTRACT REGISTER v1.0

**Status:** W0 read-only inventory evidence; not an implementation or acceptance certificate  
**Base pin:** `fd61ed335`  
**Scope:** exactly the 22 active L3 identities named by the current-state disposition; persisted domain/control outputs and typed service response payloads  
**Excluded identity:** retired `ka_gochara_sweep` (protected generation-1 corpus only; never an active producer)

## Reading rules

A **field row** is one physical persisted column or one typed service-response member. Stable nested members are separately listed for typed service objects. JSONB/map internals that the producer intentionally leaves open-ended are recorded under “Unknown and dynamic shapes”; the top-level persisted field remains an explicit row. Surrogate row IDs and wall-clock `computed_at` are excluded because accepted output-digest practice excludes them as non-content. Registry health fields are shared orchestrator control state, not service payload.

Qualification codes: **Q1** = present in an accepted `asset_output_digest_specs` component; **Q2** = source + DDL observed but no accepted output digest; **Q4** = protected candidate partition requiring generation-aware publication; **QX** = known unsafe/excluded from digest; **S1** = typed, ephemeral service contract with zero domain rows. **NK** is a natural/digest-key member; **PART** is a producer partition discriminator. Every future test identifier below is exact and intentionally absent until a W-wave implements the gate.

Primary evidence: active set and ownership map at `00_ARCHITECTURE/briefs/nirmana/MADHAV_DATA_PLANE_L3_CURRENT_STATE_AND_DISPOSITION_v1_0.md:54-61,126-145`; digest overlays at `platform/migrations/900_nirmana_l3_wave0_overlay_output_digest_specs.sql:19-57`, resonance at `platform/migrations/901_nirmana_l3_ka_gochara_resonance_output_digest_spec.sql:19-27`, Kṣetra at `platform/migrations/1002_nirmana_l3_ka_kshetra_output_digest_spec.sql:8-40,196-199`, Gochara at `platform/migrations/1018_nirmana_l3_ka_gochara_output_digest_spec.sql`, Taranga/Jīvana at `platform/migrations/1021_nirmana_l3_ka_taranga_ka_jivana_parva_output_digest_specs.sql`, Yojaka at `platform/migrations/1024_nirmana_l3_ka_yojaka_output_digest_spec.sql`, Darśana/Bhaviṣya/Saṅgam at migrations `972`, `974`, and `980`.

## Receiving-operator evidence anchors

The receiver names in the register are grounded in the actual projections, not only registry prose: Avadhi `platform/src/lib/retrieval/registry/layers/L3_kala/query_dasha_dossier.ts:89-101`; convergence `query_convergence_windows.ts:118-135`; obstruction `query_obstruction_periods.ts:79-89`; activation/predicates `query_temporal_activation.ts:185-210`; Darśana `query_temporal_view.ts:82-95`; Jīvana `query_life_arc.ts:149-162`; Bhaviṣya `query_projections.ts:251-293`; Taranga `query_activation_waveform.ts:84-128`; Kota `query_kota_chakra.ts:103-120`; Moorti `query_moorti_nirnaya.ts:99-115`; Vedha `query_vedha_gochara.ts:137-152`; Sudarśana `query_sudarshana_varsha.ts:83-97`; Tithi `query_tithi_pravesha.ts:83-99` (all under `platform/src/lib/retrieval/registry/layers/L3_kala/`). Gochara's served projection is `platform-mcp/src/tools/retrieval/register_gochara_windows.ts:604-642`. Kṣetra field consumers include `platform-mcp/src/tools/kala_views/story.ts:121-191`, `explain.ts:40`, and `ritual.ts:782-785`. Service payload contracts are their cited dataclasses and public functions; they do not have persisted retrieval projections.

## Shared-owner fences

1. **Gochara staging:** `ka_gochara` (generation 2) and `ka_gochara_v3_century_materialize` (generation 3 staging) share `kala_gochara_windows_v2`. Any DELETE/UPSERT must include chart, event class, and generation; v3 additionally partitions by era slice/resolution. Evidence: `platform/python-sidecar/pipeline/orchestrator/writers/ka_gochara.py:59,141-156`; `platform/python-sidecar/pipeline/orchestrator/writers/ka_gochara_v3_century_materialize.py:503-524`.
2. **Protected production:** retired v1 sweep rows and century-v3 rows coexist in `kala_gochara_windows`. The retired writer is excluded from the 22; generation-blind mutation is forbidden. Evidence: `platform/supabase/migrations/542_kala_gochara_windows_v2.sql:153-163`; `platform/supabase/migrations/566_parishkara_mr06_gen3_protection.sql`.
3. **Build state:** generation 2 and 3 share `kala_gochara_v2_build_state`; primary partition is `(chart_id,event_class,generation)`. Evidence: `platform/supabase/migrations/541_kala_gochara_v2_build_state.sql:69-101`.
4. **Kṣetra insights:** `ka_kshetra` owns only `kala_insights.lel_derived=false`; `mi_bhara` owns true. Digest and deletion must retain the predicate. Evidence: `platform/migrations/1002_nirmana_l3_ka_kshetra_output_digest_spec.sql:198`; `platform/python-sidecar/services/ka_kshetra/writer.py:2424`.
5. **Taranga:** the batch writer and opt-in `services/taranga_service.py::record_evidence` can UPSERT the same `(chart_id,month,scope_kind,scope_id)` key. A wave cannot claim sole-writer determinism without fencing that path. Evidence: `platform/python-sidecar/pipeline/orchestrator/writers/ka_taranga.py:56-67`; `platform/python-sidecar/services/taranga_service.py:773`.
6. **Kṣetra control surface:** the orchestrator owns shared `build_substep_progress` rows; stage modules may not expand their ownership to that relation. `kala_field_weight_versions/weights` are inputs owned outside Kṣetra, not outputs of this asset. Evidence: `platform/python-sidecar/services/ka_kshetra/writer.py:1099-1113`; orchestrator contract in `CLAUDE.md`.
7. **Bhaviṣya history:** outcome-bearing or `phala_anchors`-referenced matched rows are immutable absent a generation schema; content change fails before DML. Evidence: `platform/python-sidecar/pipeline/orchestrator/writers/ka_bhavishya_lekha.py:9-15,243-306,377-431`.

## Protected-retired exclusion

`ka_gochara_sweep` is a tombstone, not identity 23. Its generation-1 rows are a read-only benchmark and protected corpus. It has no producer rows in this register. A future field addition may not use its stale registry `has_writer` state as authority. Evidence: `platform/python-sidecar/pipeline/orchestrator/writers/ka_gochara_sweep.py`; disposition `00_ARCHITECTURE/briefs/nirmana/MADHAV_DATA_PLANE_L3_CURRENT_STATE_AND_DISPOSITION_v1_0.md:54-61,131`; retirement account `platform/migrations/972_nirmana_l3_ka_kala_darshana_output_digest_spec.sql:35-51`.

A future wave must publish JSON Schema (or typed model) before a nested member becomes a field-level compatibility promise.

- Kṣetra’s `kala_field_routes` and `kala_field_boundaries` are fully inventoried but **QX**: the accepted digest explicitly excluded them after proving reminted surrogate edge IDs and ayanamsha-ambiguous boundary selection. Evidence: `platform/migrations/1002_nirmana_l3_ka_kshetra_output_digest_spec.sql:20-38`.

## W-wave gate

No W implementation wave may begin for an asset/partition until all of the following are true:

1. its rows above are reviewed against the current DDL, actual INSERT/UPDATE projection, typed service result, accepted digest component (when any), and every receiving SELECT;
2. every **Q2**, **Q4**, or **QX** row has a recorded disposition; QX cannot be promoted by prose alone;
3. every JSON/dynamic member newly relied upon by a consumer has a schema/type and an exact falsifying test;
4. shared-owner DELETE/UPSERT predicates are mechanically proven to preserve other generations/owners;
5. the exact future tests named in affected rows exist and fail on field removal, type/nullability drift, partition widening, or receiver mismatch;
6. digest key/value columns equal the accepted spec byte-for-byte, except for an explicitly ratified spec change;
7. service assets prove zero domain DML and failure propagation, and protected-retired generation 1 remains untouched.

**Gate state at this base:** inventory complete as evidence; implementation authorization remains **CLOSED**. This register does not self-ratify source qualifications, repair Q2/Q4/QX gaps, or authorize a migration.
